---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- @app.route('/save-consultancy', methods=['POST']) def save_consultancy(): try: data = request.json or {} # Only require IDs + datetime required_fields = ['patient_id', 'doctor_id', 'appointment_datetime'] missing_fields = [ f for f in required_fields if isinstance(data.get(f), str) and not (data.get(f) or '').strip() # Check if the field is a string and then strip or (data.get(f) is None) # If the field is None, it's missing ] if missing_fields: return jsonify({ 'success': False, 'error': f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_fields)}" }), 400 # Required fields patient_id = int(data['patient_id']) doctor_id = int(data['doctor_id']) # Ensure 'appointment_datetime' is a string before calling .strip() appointment_datetime = str(data.get('appointment_datetime', '')).strip() # Optional fields (can be empty → blank letterhead) chief_complaint = (data.get('chief_complaint') or '').strip() diagnosis = (data.get('diagnosis') or '').strip() prescription = (data.get('prescription') or '').strip() additional_notes = (data.get('additional_notes') or '').strip() patient_email = (data.get('patient_email') or '').strip() # Save to DB conn = get_db() cur = conn.cursor() cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO consultancy_records (patient_id, doctor_id, chief_complaint, diagnosis, prescription, additional_notes, appointment_datetime) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s) """, ( patient_id, doctor_id, chief_complaint, diagnosis, prescription, additional_notes, appointment_datetime )) consultancy_id = cur.lastrowid conn.commit() cur.close() conn.close() # Email to patient (optional) if patient_email: try: subject = "Your Appointment Details - Hospital" mail_body = ( f"Dear Patient,\n\n" f"You have a new appointment scheduled.\n" f"Appointment Date & Time: {appointment_datetime}\n" f"Chief Complaint: {chief_complaint}\n" f"Diagnosis: {diagnosis}\n" f"Prescription: {prescription}\n" f"Additional Notes: {additional_notes}\n\n" f"Regards,\nHospital Team" ) msg = Message(subject, recipients=[patient_email], body=mail_body) mail.send(msg) except Exception as e: print("Email error:", e) return jsonify({ 'success': True, 'consultancy_id': consultancy_id }) except Exception as e: print("UNEXPECTED ERROR:", e) return jsonify({ 'success': False, 'error': str(e) }), 500 # Your OTP is: {otp} This OTP is valid for 10 minutes. If you did not request this, please ignore this email. Regards, Hospital Management System """ send_email(email, subject, body) flash("OTP has been sent to your email.", "success") except Exception as e: print("Error sending OTP email:", e) flash("Failed to send OTP email.", "warning") return render_template("receptionist_nurse_forgot_password.html", email=email) # 2) RESET PASSWORD USING OTP elif action == "reset_password": email = request.form.get("email", "").strip().lower() otp = request.form.get("otp", "").strip() new_pass = request.form.get("new_password", "").strip() confirm_pass = request.form.get("confirm_password", "").strip() if not (email and otp and new_pass and confirm_pass): flash("Please fill in all fields.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_nurse_forgot_password.html", email=email) if new_pass != confirm_pass: flash("New password and confirm password do not match.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_nurse_forgot_password.html", email=email) # check data saved in session sess_email = session.get("receptionist_nurse_fp_email") sess_otp = session.get("receptionist_nurse_fp_otp") sess_time = session.get("receptionist_nurse_fp_time") if not (sess_email and sess_otp and sess_time): flash("OTP session expired or not requested. Please request OTP again.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_nurse_forgot_password.html", email=email) if email != sess_email: flash("This email does not match the one used to request OTP.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_nurse_forgot_password.html", email=email) if time.time() - sess_time > 600: flash("OTP has expired. """, (staff["id"],)) # con.commit() # return redirect(url_for(dashboard_endpoint)) import uuid from datetime import datetime, timedelta def update_user_status(user_id, user_type, status='online', session_token=None): """Update user login status and session tracking""" con = get_db() cur = con.cursor() try: # Update user status in respective table table_map = { 'hr': 'hr_managers', 'admin': 'admins', 'staff': 'staff_members', 'pharmacy_admin': 'pharmacy_admins' } table = table_map.get(user_type) if not table: return False if status == 'online': cur.execute(f""" UPDATE {table} SET login_status = %s, last_login = NOW(), is_active = 1 WHERE id = %s """, (status, user_id)) else: cur.execute(f""" UPDATE {table} SET login_status = %s, last_logout = NOW(), is_active = 0 WHERE id = %s """, (status, user_id)) # Handle session tracking if status == 'online' and session_token: # Create new session cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO user_sessions (user_id, user_type, session_token, ip_address, user_agent) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s) """, (user_id, user_type, session_token, request.environ.get('REMOTE_ADDR'), request.environ.get('HTTP_USER_AGENT'))) elif status == 'offline' and session_token: # Close session cur.execute(""" UPDATE user_sessions SET logout_time = NOW(), is_active = FALSE WHERE session_token = %s """, (session_token,)) con.commit() return True except Exception as e: print(f"Status update error: {e}") con.rollback() return False finally: con.close() # run_id = cur.lastrowid # con.commit() # con.close() # # store path in session (simple + works for local dev) # session["import_file_path"] = path # flash("File uploaded. Now map columns.", "success") # return redirect(url_for("pharmacy_import_map", run_id=run_id)) # ----------------------------- # Helpers # ----------------------------- def _read_headers_from_file(path: str): ext = os.path.splitext(path)[1].lower() if ext == ".xlsx": wb = load_workbook(path, data_only=True) ws = wb.active headers = [] for cell in ws[1]: if cell.value is None: headers.append("") else: headers.append(str(cell.value).strip()) return headers if ext == ".csv": with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f: reader = csv.reader(f) headers = next(reader, []) return [h.strip() for h in headers] raise ValueError("Unsupported file type") def _parse_date(val): """Accepts excel date, 'YYYY-MM-DD', 'DD-MM-YYYY', 'DD/MM/YYYY' etc.""" if val is None or val == "": return None if isinstance(val, (datetime, date)): return val.date() if isinstance(val, datetime) else val s = str(val).strip() for fmt in ("%Y-%m-%d", "%d-%m-%Y", "%d/%m/%Y", "%d.%m.%Y"): try: return datetime.strptime(s, fmt).date() except Exception: pass return None def _to_int(val, default=0): try: if val is None or str(val).strip() == "": return default return int(float(str(val).strip())) except Exception: return default def _to_float(val, default=0.0): try: if val is None or str(val).strip() == "": return default return float(str(val).strip()) except Exception: return default def _read_rows_as_dicts(path: str, headers: list[str]): """Returns list of dicts: {header: cell_value}""" ext = os.path.splitext(path)[1].lower() if ext == ".xlsx": wb = load_workbook(path, data_only=True) ws = wb.active rows = [] for r in ws.iter_rows(min_row=2, values_only=True): row = {} for i, h in enumerate(headers): if not h: continue row[h] = r[i] if i < len(r) else None rows.append(row) return rows if ext == ".csv": with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f: reader = csv.DictReader(f) return list(reader) raise ValueError("Unsupported file type") # ----------------------------- # IMPORT: Mapping screen # ----------------------------- @app.route("/pharmacy/import/map/", methods=["GET", "POST"]) def pharmacy_import_map(run_id): if not require_pharmacy_login(): return redirect("/pharmacy-admin-login") hospital_id = session["hospital_id"] path = session.get("import_file_path") if not path or not os.path.exists(path): flash("Upload file again. Import file not found.", "danger") return redirect(url_for("pharmacy_import")) con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # verify run belongs to hospital cur.execute(""" SELECT id, template_id FROM pharmacy_import_runs WHERE id=%s AND hospital_id=%s """, (run_id, hospital_id)) run = cur.fetchone() if not run: con.close() flash("Import run not found.", "danger") return redirect(url_for("pharmacy_import")) template_id = run["template_id"] headers = _read_headers_from_file(path) canonical_fields = [ ("product_name", True), ("batch_no", True), ("expiry_date", False), ("qty", True), ("purchase_rate", False), ("mrp", False), ("selling_price", False), ] if request.method == "POST": # wipe old mappings for this template (so user can re-map) cur2 = con.cursor() cur2.execute("DELETE FROM pharmacy_import_mappings WHERE template_id=%s", (template_id,)) for cf, required in canonical_fields: vendor_col = (request.form.get(cf) or "").strip() if vendor_col: cur2.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_import_mappings (template_id, canonical_field, vendor_column_name, transform_rule, is_required) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,NULL,%s) """, (template_id, cf, vendor_col, 1 if required else 0)) con.commit() con.close() flash("Mapping saved. Importing now...", "success") return redirect(url_for("pharmacy_import_process", run_id=run_id)) # load existing mappings to pre-fill dropdowns cur.execute(""" SELECT canonical_field, vendor_column_name FROM pharmacy_import_mappings WHERE template_id=%s """, (template_id,)) existing = {r["canonical_field"]: r["vendor_column_name"] for r in cur.fetchall()} con.close() return render_template( "pharmacy_import_map.html", run_id=run_id, headers=headers, canonical_fields=canonical_fields, existing=existing ) # ----------------------------- # IMPORT: Process (OPENING stock) # ----------------------------- @app.route("/pharmacy/import/process/") def pharmacy_import_process(run_id): if not require_pharmacy_login(): return redirect("/pharmacy-admin-login") hospital_id = session["hospital_id"] pharmacy_admin_id = session["pharmacy_admin_id"] path = session.get("import_file_path") if not path or not os.path.exists(path): flash("Upload file again. Import file not found.", "danger") return redirect(url_for("pharmacy_import")) con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) cur2 = con.cursor() # run + template cur.execute(""" SELECT r.id, r.template_id FROM pharmacy_import_runs r WHERE r.id=%s AND r.hospital_id=%s """, (run_id, hospital_id)) run = cur.fetchone() if not run: con.close() flash("Import run not found.", "danger") return redirect(url_for("pharmacy_import")) template_id = run["template_id"] # mappings cur.execute(""" SELECT canonical_field, vendor_column_name, is_required FROM pharmacy_import_mappings WHERE template_id=%s """, (template_id,)) maps = cur.fetchall() mapping = {m["canonical_field"]: m["vendor_column_name"] for m in maps} required_fields = [m["canonical_field"] for m in maps if m["is_required"] == 1] # we require at least these for must in ["product_name", "batch_no", "qty"]: if must not in mapping: con.close() flash(f"Mapping missing: {must}. Please map columns first.", "danger") return redirect(url_for("pharmacy_import_map", run_id=run_id)) headers = _read_headers_from_file(path) rows = _read_rows_as_dicts(path, headers) total_rows = len(rows) success = 0 failed = 0 # clear previous errors for this run cur2.execute(""" DELETE FROM pharmacy_import_errors WHERE run_id=%s """, (run_id,)) def err(row_no, msg, raw): nonlocal failed failed += 1 cur2.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_import_errors (run_id, row_no, error_message, raw_json) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s) """, (run_id, row_no, msg, json.dumps(raw, default=str))) try: for idx, raw in enumerate(rows, start=2): # row no in excel (header=1) # extract mapped values product_name = (raw.get(mapping["product_name"]) or "").strip() batch_no = (raw.get(mapping["batch_no"]) or "").strip() qty = _to_int(raw.get(mapping["qty"]), 0) expiry_date = _parse_date(raw.get(mapping.get("expiry_date", ""), None)) purchase_rate = _to_float(raw.get(mapping.get("purchase_rate", ""), None), 0.0) mrp = _to_float(raw.get(mapping.get("mrp", ""), None), 0.0) selling_price = _to_float(raw.get(mapping.get("selling_price", ""), None), 0.0) # validate required if not product_name: err(idx, "Missing product_name", raw); continue if not batch_no: err(idx, "Missing batch_no", raw); continue if qty <= 0: err(idx, "Invalid qty (must be > 0)", raw); continue # 1) upsert product (hospital_id + product_name is unique) cur.execute(""" SELECT id FROM pharmacy_products WHERE hospital_id=%s AND product_name=%s LIMIT 1 """, (hospital_id, product_name)) pr = cur.fetchone() if pr: product_id = pr["id"] else: cur2.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_products (hospital_id, product_name, reorder_level, is_active) VALUES (%s,%s,0,1) """, (hospital_id, product_name)) product_id = cur2.lastrowid # 2) upsert batch cur.execute(""" SELECT id FROM pharmacy_stock_batches WHERE hospital_id=%s AND product_id=%s AND batch_no=%s LIMIT 1 """, (hospital_id, product_id, batch_no)) b = cur.fetchone() if b: batch_id = b["id"] cur2.execute(""" UPDATE pharmacy_stock_batches SET qty_on_hand = qty_on_hand + %s, expiry_date = COALESCE(%s, expiry_date), purchase_rate = CASE WHEN %s>0 THEN %s ELSE purchase_rate END, mrp = CASE WHEN %s>0 THEN %s ELSE mrp END, selling_price = CASE WHEN %s>0 THEN %s ELSE selling_price END WHERE id=%s AND hospital_id=%s """, (qty, expiry_date, purchase_rate, purchase_rate, mrp, mrp, selling_price, selling_price, batch_id, hospital_id)) else: cur2.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_stock_batches (hospital_id, product_id, batch_no, expiry_date, qty_on_hand, purchase_rate, mrp, selling_price) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s) """, (hospital_id, product_id, batch_no, expiry_date, qty, purchase_rate, mrp, selling_price)) batch_id = cur2.lastrowid # 3) ledger entry as OPENING (import) cur2.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_stock_ledger (hospital_id, product_id, batch_id, txn_type, ref_table, ref_id, qty_in, qty_out, note) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,'OPENING','pharmacy_import_runs',%s,%s,0,%s) """, (hospital_id, product_id, batch_id, run_id, qty, f"Opening import run #{run_id}")) success += 1 # update run counts cur2.execute(""" UPDATE pharmacy_import_runs SET total_rows=%s, success_rows=%s, failed_rows=%s WHERE id=%s AND hospital_id=%s """, (total_rows, success, failed, run_id, hospital_id)) con.commit() con.close() flash(f"Import completed. Success: {success}, Failed: {failed}", "success") return redirect(url_for("pharmacy_import_errors_view", run_id=run_id)) except Exception as e: con.rollback() con.close() flash(f"Import failed: {e}", "danger") return redirect(url_for("pharmacy_import_map", run_id=run_id)) # ----------------------------- # IMPORT: View errors # ----------------------------- @app.route("/pharmacy/import/errors/") def pharmacy_import_errors_view(run_id): if not require_pharmacy_login(): return redirect("/pharmacy-admin-login") hospital_id = session["hospital_id"] con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) cur.execute(""" SELECT * FROM pharmacy_import_errors WHERE run_id=%s ORDER BY row_no ASC """, (run_id,)) errors = cur.fetchall() cur.execute(""" SELECT total_rows, success_rows, failed_rows, file_name FROM pharmacy_import_runs WHERE id=%s AND hospital_id=%s """, (run_id, hospital_id)) run = cur.fetchone() con.close() return render_template("pharmacy_import_errors.html", errors=errors, run=run) import os from werkzeug.utils import secure_filename @app.route("/pharmacy/import/upload", methods=["POST"]) def pharmacy_import_upload(): if not require_pharmacy_login(): return redirect("/pharmacy-admin-login") hospital_id = session["hospital_id"] template_id = request.form.get('template_id') file_type = request.form.get('file_type') vendor_id = request.form.get('vendor_id') or None if 'file' not in request.files: flash('No file selected', 'danger') return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import')) file = request.files['file'] if file.filename == '': flash('No file selected', 'danger') return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import')) if not file_type: flash('Please select file type', 'danger') return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import')) # Create temp directory temp_dir = 'temp_uploads' if not os.path.exists(temp_dir): os.makedirs(temp_dir) # Save file temporarily filename = secure_filename(file.filename) temp_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, filename) file.save(temp_path) # Store in session session['upload_info'] = { 'filename': filename, 'temp_path': temp_path, 'file_type': file_type, 'vendor_id': vendor_id, 'hospital_id': hospital_id } if template_id: # Use existing template flash('Using existing template (preview coming soon)', 'info') return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import')) else: # No template - go to mapping return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import_mapping')) @app.route("/pharmacy/import/preview/") def pharmacy_import_preview(template_id): # This route will show preview of parsed data using the template flash('Preview functionality coming soon!', 'info') return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import')) @app.route("/pharmacy/import/mapping", methods=["GET", "POST"]) def pharmacy_import_mapping(): if not redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import')) if request.method == 'GET': try: # Read file to get headers and sample data file_path = upload_info['temp_path'] file_type = upload_info['file_type'] if file_type == 'CSV': import pandas as pd df = pd.read_csv(file_path, nrows=5) # Read first 5 rows else: # XLSX import pandas as pd df = pd.read_excel(file_path, nrows=5) headers = df.columns.tolist() sample_data = df.to_dict('records') # System fields to map to system_fields = [ {'field': 'product_name', 'label': 'Product Name', 'required': True}, {'field': 'batch_no', 'label': 'Batch Number', 'required': True}, {'field': 'expiry_date', 'label': 'Expiry Date', 'required': False}, {'field': 'qty', 'label': 'Quantity', 'required': True}, {'field': 'mrp', 'label': 'MRP/Selling Price', 'required': True}, {'field': 'purchase_rate', 'label': 'Purchase Price', 'required': True}, {'field': 'hsn_code', 'label': 'HSN Code', 'required': False}, {'field': 'manufacturer', 'label': 'Manufacturer', 'required': False}, ] return render_template('pharmacy_import_mapping.html', headers=headers, sample_data=sample_data, system_fields=system_fields, upload_info=upload_info) except Exception as e: flash(f'Error reading file: {str(e)}', 'danger') return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import')) # POST - Save the mapping else: # POST - Save mapping AND process data template_name = request.form.get('template_name') if not template_name: flash('Template name is required', 'danger') return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import_mapping')) # Collect field mappings field_mappings = {} for key in request.form: if key.startswith('field_'): system_field = key[6:] vendor_column = request.form[key] if vendor_column and vendor_column != '': field_mappings[system_field] = vendor_column if not field_mappings: flash('Please map at least one field', 'danger') return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import_mapping')) try: # Save template to database con = get_db() cur = con.cursor() cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_import_templates (hospital_id, vendor_id, template_name, file_type, header_row, is_active) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, 1, 1) """, (upload_info['hospital_id'], upload_info['vendor_id'], template_name, upload_info['file_type'])) template_id = cur.lastrowid # Save field mappings for system_field, vendor_column in field_mappings.items(): cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_import_mappings (template_id, canonical_field, vendor_column_name, is_required) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, 1) """, (template_id, system_field, vendor_column)) con.commit() # *** NEW: Process the actual data *** success_count, error_count, errors = process_import_data( upload_info['temp_path'], upload_info['file_type'], template_id, upload_info['hospital_id'], upload_info['vendor_id'] ) con.close() flash(f'Template "{template_name}" created successfully!', 'success') flash(f'Processed: {success_count} items added, {error_count} errors', 'info') if errors: for error in errors[:5]: # Show first 5 errors flash(f'Error: {error}', 'warning') # Clean up temp file if os.path.exists(upload_info['temp_path']): os.remove(upload_info['temp_path']) # Clear session session.pop('upload_info', None) return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_stock')) # Redirect to stock to see results except Exception as e: flash(f'Error saving template: {str(e)}', 'danger') return redirect(url_for('pharmacy_import_mapping')) def process_import_data(file_path, file_type, template_id, hospital_id, vendor_id): """Process uploaded file data using the mapping template""" success_count = 0 error_count = 0 errors = [] try: # Get template mappings con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) cur.execute(""" SELECT canonical_field, vendor_column_name FROM pharmacy_import_mappings WHERE template_id = %s """, (template_id,)) mappings = {row['canonical_field']: row['vendor_column_name'] for row in cur.fetchall()} # Read the file if file_type == 'CSV': import pandas as pd df = pd.read_csv(file_path) else: # XLSX import pandas as pd df = pd.read_excel(file_path) # Process each row for index, row in df.iterrows(): try: # Extract mapped values product_name = row.get(mappings.get('product_name', ''), '').strip() batch_no = str(row.get(mappings.get('batch_no', ''), '')).strip() qty = int(float(row.get(mappings.get('qty', ''), 0) or 0)) # Optional fields expiry_date = row.get(mappings.get('expiry_date'), None) if expiry_date and expiry_date != '' and pd.notna(expiry_date): if isinstance(expiry_date, str): # Try to parse date string from datetime import datetime try: expiry_date = datetime.strptime(expiry_date, '%Y-%m-%d').date() except: try: expiry_date = datetime.strptime(expiry_date, '%d/%m/%Y').date() except: expiry_date = None else: expiry_date = None mrp = float(row.get(mappings.get('mrp', ''), 0) or 0) purchase_rate = float(row.get(mappings.get('purchase_rate', ''), 0) or 0) # Validate required fields if not product_name or not batch_no or qty <= 0: errors.append(f'Row {index + 2}: Missing required fields (product_name, batch_no, qty)') error_count += 1 continue # Find or create product cur.execute(""" SELECT id FROM pharmacy_products WHERE hospital_id = %s AND product_name = %s LIMIT 1 """, (hospital_id, product_name)) product = cur.fetchone() if not product: # Create new product cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_products (hospital_id, product_name, generic_name, brand_name, unit, is_active) VALUES (%s, %s, '', '', 'PCS', 1) """, (hospital_id, product_name)) product_id = cur.lastrowid else: product_id = product['id'] # Check if batch exists cur.execute(""" SELECT id, qty_on_hand FROM pharmacy_stock_batches WHERE hospital_id = %s AND product_id = %s AND batch_no = %s """, (hospital_id, product_id, batch_no)) existing_batch = cur.fetchone() if existing_batch: # Update existing batch batch_id = existing_batch['id'] cur.execute(""" UPDATE pharmacy_stock_batches SET qty_on_hand = qty_on_hand + %s, expiry_date = COALESCE(%s, expiry_date), mrp = COALESCE(NULLIF(%s, 0), mrp), purchase_rate = COALESCE(NULLIF(%s, 0), purchase_rate) WHERE id = %s """, (qty, expiry_date, mrp, purchase_rate, batch_id)) else: # Create new batch cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_stock_batches (hospital_id, product_id, batch_no, expiry_date, qty_on_hand, purchase_rate, mrp, selling_price) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s) """, (hospital_id, product_id, batch_no, expiry_date, qty, purchase_rate, mrp, mrp)) # Using MRP as selling price batch_id = cur.lastrowid # Add stock ledger entry cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_stock_ledger (hospital_id, product_id, batch_id, txn_type, ref_table, ref_id, qty_in, qty_out, note) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, 'IMPORT', 'pharmacy_import_templates', %s, %s, 0, %s) """, (hospital_id, product_id, batch_id, template_id, qty, f'Import from file: {os.path.basename(file_path)}')) success_count += 1 except Exception as e: errors.append(f'Row {index + 2}: {str(e)}') error_count += 1 continue con.commit() con.close() except Exception as e: errors.append(f'File processing error: {str(e)}') error_count += 1 return @app.route("/edit-hospital-admin/", methods=["GET", "POST"]) @app.route("/edit-hospital-admin/", methods=["GET", "POST"]) def edit_hospital_admin(id): if "superadmin" not in session: return redirect("/") con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # If hospital name/address left blank, keep old values if not hospital_name: hospital_name = record["hospital_name"] if not hospital_address: hospital_address = record["hospital_address"] if password_raw: rep_password_hashed = generate_password_hash(password_raw) cur.execute(""" UPDATE hospital_representatives SET hospital_name=%s, hospital_address=%s, rep_name=%s, rep_gender=%s, rep_email=%s, rep_phone=%s, rep_username=%s, rep_password=%s WHERE id=%s """, ( hospital_name, hospital_address, rep_name, rep_gender, rep_email, rep_phone, rep_username, rep_password_hashed, id )) else: cur.execute(""" UPDATE hospital_representatives SET hospital_name=%s, hospital_address=%s, rep_name=%s, rep_gender=%s, rep_email=%s, rep_phone=%s, rep_username=%s WHERE id=%s """, ( hospital_name, hospital_address, rep_name, rep_gender, rep_email, rep_phone, rep_username, id )) con.commit() flash("Record updated successfully!", "success") return redirect("/hospital-admins") # HR-related routes @app.route("/rep-add-hr", methods=["GET", "POST"]) def rep_add_hr(): if "rep_id" not in session: return redirect("/rep-login") rep_id = session["rep_id"] con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # Get representative's hospital info cur.execute(""" SELECT hr.hospital_id, h.hospital_name, h.hospital_mode FROM hospital_representatives hr JOIN hospitals h ON hr.hospital_id = h.id WHERE hr.id = %s """, (rep_id,)) rep_data = cur.fetchone() if not rep_data or rep_data.get('hospital_mode') != 'ENTERPRISE': flash("HR management is only available for Enterprise mode hospitals.", "error") return redirect("/rep-dashboard") if request.method == "POST": hr_name = request.form.get("hr_name", "").strip() hr_gender = request.form.get("hr_gender") hr_email = request.form.get("hr_email", "").strip() hr_phone = request.form.get("hr_phone", "").strip() hr_username = request.form.get("hr_username", "").strip() hr_password = request.form.get("hr_password", "").strip() # Hash password hashed_password = generate_password_hash(hr_password) # Insert HR Manager cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO hr_managers (hospital_id, hr_name, hr_gender, hr_email, hr_phone, hr_username, hr_password, created_by_rep_id) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s) """, ( rep_data['hospital_id'], hr_name, hr_gender, hr_email, hr_phone, hr_username, hashed_password, rep_id )) con.commit() # Login URL: {login_url} Username: {hr_username} Password: {hr_password} Please login and change your password after first login. Regards, Hospital Management System """ send_email(hr_email, subject, body) flash("HR Manager added successfully and credentials sent to email!", "success") except Exception as e: print("Error sending HR email:", e) flash("HR Manager added, but failed to send email.", "warning") con.close() return redirect("/rep-dashboard") con.close() return render_template("rep_add_hr.html", hospital_name=rep_data['hospital_name']) @app.route("/rep-view-hr") def rep_view_hr(): if "rep_id" not in session: return redirect("/rep-login") rep_id = session["rep_id"] con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # Get representative's hospital info cur.execute(""" SELECT hr.hospital_id, h.hospital_name, h.hospital_mode FROM hospital_representatives hr JOIN hospitals h ON hr.hospital_id = h.id WHERE hr.id = %s """, (rep_id,)) rep_data = cur.fetchone() if not rep_data or rep_data.get("hospital_mode") != "ENTERPRISE": con.close() flash("HR management is only available for Enterprise mode hospitals.", "error") return redirect("/rep-dashboard") # Edit HR Manager Route @app.route('/rep-edit-hr/', methods=['GET', 'POST']) def rep_edit_hr(hr_id): if "rep_id" not in session: return redirect("/rep-login") rep_id = session["rep_id"] con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) try: # Verify HR belongs to this representative cur.execute(""" SELECT hr.*, h.hospital_name, h.hospital_mode FROM hr_managers hr JOIN hospitals h ON hr.hospital_id = h.id WHERE hr.id = %s AND hr.created_by_rep_id = %s """, (hr_id, rep_id)) hr_data = cur.fetchone() if not hr_data: con.close() flash("HR Manager not found or access denied.", "error") return redirect("/rep-view-hr") if hr_data.get("hospital_mode") != "ENTERPRISE": con.close() flash("HR management is only available for Enterprise mode hospitals.", "error") return redirect("/rep-dashboard") if request.method == 'POST': # Get form data hr_name = request.form.get('hr_name', '').strip() hr_gender = request.form.get('hr_gender', '') hr_email = request.form.get('hr_email', '').strip().lower() hr_phone = request.form.get('hr_phone', '').strip() hr_username = request.form.get('hr_username', '').strip() new_password = request.form.get('new_password', '').strip() can_login = 1 if request.form.get('can_login') else 0 # Validation if not all([hr_name, hr_gender, hr_email, hr_phone, hr_username]): flash("All fields are required.", "error") return render_template("rep_edit_hr.html", hr=hr_data) if hr_gender not in ['Male', 'Female', 'Other']: flash("Invalid gender selection.", "error") return render_template("rep_edit_hr.html", hr=hr_data) # Check for duplicate email (excluding current HR) cur.execute(""" SELECT id FROM hr_managers WHERE hr_email = %s AND id != %s """, (hr_email, hr_id)) if cur.fetchone(): flash("Email already exists for another HR manager.", "error") return render_template("rep_edit_hr.html", hr=hr_data) # Check for duplicate username (excluding current HR) cur.execute(""" SELECT id FROM hr_managers WHERE hr_username = %s AND id != %s """, (hr_username, hr_id)) if cur.fetchone(): flash("Username already exists for another HR manager.", "error") return render_template("rep_edit_hr.html", hr=hr_data) # Prepare update query if new_password: # Hash new password if provided hashed_password = generate_password_hash(new_password) update_query = """ UPDATE hr_managers SET hr_name = %s, hr_gender = %s, hr_email = %s, hr_phone = %s, hr_username = %s, hr_password = %s, can_login = %s WHERE id = %s """ cur.execute(update_query, (hr_name, hr_gender, hr_email, hr_phone, hr_username, hashed_password, can_login, hr_id)) else: # Update without changing password update_query = """ UPDATE hr_managers SET hr_name = %s, hr_gender = %s, hr_email = %s, hr_phone = %s, hr_username = %s, can_login = %s WHERE id = %s """ cur.execute(update_query, (hr_name, hr_gender, hr_email, hr_phone, hr_username, can_login, hr_id)) con.commit() con.close() flash("HR Manager updated successfully!", "success") return redirect("/rep-view-hr") con.close() return render_template("rep_edit_hr.html", hr=hr_data) except Exception as e: con.rollback() con.close() print(f"Edit HR error: {e}") flash(f"Error updating HR Manager: {str(e)}", "error") return redirect("/rep-view-hr") # Toggle HR Status Route @app.route('/rep-toggle-hr-status//') def rep_toggle_hr_status(hr_id, action): if "rep_id" not in session: return redirect("/rep-login") rep_id = session["rep_id"] con = get_db() cur = con.cursor() try: # Verify HR belongs to this representative cur.execute(""" SELECT id FROM hr_managers WHERE id = %s AND created_by_rep_id = %s """, (hr_id, rep_id)) if not cur.fetchone(): con.close() flash("HR Manager not found or access denied.", "error") return redirect("/rep-view-hr") # Toggle status if action == 'enable': cur.execute("UPDATE hr_managers SET is_active = 1 WHERE id = %s", (hr_id,)) flash("HR Manager enabled successfully!", "success") elif action == 'disable': cur.execute("UPDATE hr_managers SET is_active = 0 WHERE id = %s", (hr_id,)) flash("HR Manager disabled successfully!", "success") else: flash("Invalid action.", "error") return redirect("/rep-view-hr") con.commit() con.close() return redirect("/rep-view-hr") except Exception as e: con.rollback() con.close() print(f"Toggle HR status error: {e}") flash(f"Error updating HR status: {str(e)}", "error") return redirect("/rep-view-hr") # Delete HR Manager Route @app.route('/rep-delete-hr/') def rep_delete_hr(hr_id): if "rep_id" not in session: return redirect("/rep-login") rep_id = session["rep_id"] con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) try: # Verify HR belongs to this representative cur.execute(""" SELECT hr_name FROM hr_managers WHERE id = %s AND created_by_rep_id = %s """, (hr_id, rep_id)) hr_data = cur.fetchone() if not hr_data: con.close() flash("HR Manager not found or access denied.", "error") return redirect("/rep-view-hr") # Check if HR has created any departments cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as dept_count FROM departments WHERE created_by_hr_id = %s", (hr_id,)) dept_count = cur.fetchone()['dept_count'] if dept_count > 0: con.close() flash(f"Cannot delete HR Manager '{hr_data['hr_name']}' because they have created {dept_count} department(s). Please reassign or delete those departments first.", "error") return redirect("/rep-view-hr") # Check if HR has created any pharmacy admins cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as pa_count FROM pharmacy_admins WHERE created_by_hr_id = %s", (hr_id,)) pa_count = cur.fetchone()['pa_count'] if pa_count > 0: con.close() flash(f"Cannot delete HR Manager '{hr_data['hr_name']}' because they have created {pa_count} pharmacy admin(s). Create pagination info def get_page_range(current_page, total_pages, max_pages=5): """Generate page range with ellipsis for large page counts""" if total_pages <= max_pages: return list(range(1, total_pages + 1)) if current_page <= 3: return list(range(1, 4)) + ['...', total_pages] elif current_page >= total_pages - 2: return [1, '...'] + list(range(total_pages - 2, total_pages + 1)) else: return [1, '...', current_page - 1, current_page, current_page + 1, '...', total_pages] pagination_info = { 'current_page': page, 'per_page': per_page, 'total_pages': total_pages, 'total_records': total_departments, 'start_record': offset + 1 if total_departments > 0 else 0, 'end_record': min(offset + per_page, total_departments), 'page_range': get_page_range(page, total_pages) if total_pages > admin_type == 'all' # Fetch both types but limit to show preview (e.g., 5 each for 'all' view) preview_limit = 5 if admin_type == 'all' else per_page # ✅ Get department admins using existing admins table cur.execute(""" SELECT a.*, d.department_name FROM admins a LEFT JOIN departments d ON a.department_id = d.id WHERE a.hospital_id = %s ORDER BY a.created_at DESC LIMIT %s """, (hospital_id, preview_limit)) dept_admins = cur.fetchall() # Calculate start and end pages start = max(1, current_page - max_pages // 2) end = min(total_pages, start + max_pages - 1) # Adjust start if end is at maximum start = max(1, end - max_pages + 1) page_range = list(range(start, end + 1)) # Add ellipsis at the beginning if needed if start > 1: if start > 2: page_range = [1, '...'] + page_range else: page_range = [1] + page_range # Add ellipsis at the end if needed if end < total_pages: if end < total_pages - 1: page_range = page_range + ['...', total_pages] else: page_range = page_range + [total_pages] return page_range @app.route("/hr-view-department-admins") def hr_view_department_admins(): if "hr_id" not in session: flash("Session expired. @app.route("/pharmacy/stock") def pharmacy_stock(): if not require_pharmacy_login(): return redirect("/pharmacy-admin-login") hospital_id = session["hospital_id"] q = (request.args.get("q") or "").strip() con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) params = [hospital_id] q_filter = "" if q: q_filter = " AND (p.product_name LIKE %s OR p.generic_name LIKE %s OR p.brand_name LIKE %s) " params += [f"%{q}%", f"%{q}%", f"%{q}%"] cur.execute(f""" SELECT p.id AS product_id, p.product_name, p.reorder_level, COALESCE(SUM(b.qty_on_hand),0) AS total_qty, COUNT(CASE WHEN b.qty_on_hand > 0 THEN 1 END) AS active_batches, MIN(CASE WHEN b.qty_on_hand > 0 THEN b.expiry_date END) AS nearest_expiry FROM pharmacy_products p LEFT JOIN pharmacy_stock_batches b ON b.hospital_id=p.hospital_id AND b.product_id=p.id WHERE p.hospital_id=%s AND p.is_active=1 {q_filter} GROUP BY p.id, p.product_name, p.reorder_level ORDER BY p.product_name ASC """, tuple(params)) rows = cur.fetchall() con.close() return render_template("pharmacy_stock.html", rows=rows, q=q) @app.route("/pharmacy/alerts/low-stock") def pharmacy_low_stock_alerts(): if not require_pharmacy_login(): return p.is_active=1 GROUP BY p.id, p.product_name, p.reorder_level ) t WHERE t.reorder_level > 0 AND t.total_qty < t.reorder_level ORDER BY (t.total_qty*1.0 / NULLIF(t.reorder_level,0)) ASC, t.product_name "").strip() invoice_date = request.form.get("invoice_date") or None product_ids = request.form.getlist("product_id[]") batch_nos = request.form.getlist("batch_no[]") expiries = request.form.getlist("expiry_date[]") qtys = request.form.getlist("qty[]") purchase_rates = request.form.getlist("purchase_rate[]") mrps = request.form.getlist("mrp[]") sell_prices = 0.0 tax_total = 0.0 for i in range(len(product_ids)): pid = product_ids[i] bno = (batch_nos[i] or "").strip() exp = expiries[i] or None qty = int(qtys[i] or 0) pr = float(purchase_rates[i] or 0) mrp = sp, batch_id, hospital_id)) else: cur2.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_stock_batches (hospital_id, product_id, batch_no, expiry_date, qty_on_hand, purchase_rate, mrp, selling_price) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s) """, (hospital_id, pid, bno, exp, qty, pr, mrp, sp)) batch_id = cur2.lastrowid line_base = qty * pr line_tax = (line_base * tp) / 100.0 line_total = line_base + line_tax subtotal += line_base tax_total += line_tax # items cur2.execute(""" INSERT INTO pharmacy_purchase_items (purchase_id, product_id, batch_id, batch_no, expiry_date, qty, purchase_rate, mrp, pharmacy_stock_ledger (hospital_id, product_id, batch_id, txn_type, ref_table, ref_id, qty_in, qty_out, note) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,'PURCHASE','pharmacy_purchases',%s,%s,0,%s) """, (hospital_id, pid, batch_id, purchase_id, qty, f"Purchase {invoice_no}".strip())) grand_total = subtotal + tax_total cur2.execute(""" UPDATE pharmacy_purchases SET subtotal=%s, tax_total=%s, grand_total=%s WHERE id=%s AND hospital_id=%s """, (subtotal, tax_total, grand_total, purchase_id, hospital_id)) con.commit() con.close() flash("Purchase saved successfully.", "success") return pharmacy_batch_adjust(batch_id): if not require_pharmacy_login(): return redirect("/pharmacy-admin-login") hospital_id = session["hospital_id"] direction = request.form.get("direction") # IN / OUT qty = int(request.form.get("qty") or 0) note = (request.form.get("note") or "").strip() if direction not in ("IN", "OUT") or qty <= 0: flash("Invalid adjustment.", "danger") return (%s,%s,%s,'ADJUSTMENT','pharmacy_stock_batches',%s,%s,%s,%s) """, (hospital_id, product_id, batch_id, batch_id, qty_in, qty_out, note or f"Manual adjustment {direction}")) con.commit() con.close() ============================ @app.route("/forgot-password", methods=["GET", "POST"]) def admin_forgot_password(): con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # pre-fill email if we stored it in session earlier email_prefill = session.get("admin_fp_email", "") if request.method == "POST": action = request.form.get("action") # 1) SEND OTP if action == "send_otp": email = request.form.get("email", "").strip().lower() if not email: flash("Please enter your email.", "danger") return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) # find admin by email cur.execute(""" SELECT * FROM admins WHERE LOWER(admin_email) = %s """, (email,)) admin = cur.fetchone() if not admin: flash("No admin account found with that email.", "danger") return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) # generate 6-digit OTP otp = str(random.randint(100000, 999999)) # store OTP + email + timestamp in session (valid for 10 minutes) session["admin_fp_email"] = email session["admin_fp_otp"] = otp session["admin_fp_time"] = time.time() # send OTP email try: subject = "Admin Password Reset OTP" body = f""" Dear {admin['admin_name']}, You requested to reset your admin password. Your OTP is: {otp} This OTP is valid for 10 minutes. If you did not request this, please ignore this email. Regards, Hospital Management System """ send_email(email, subject, body) flash("OTP has been sent to your email.", "success") except Exception as e: print("Error sending admin OTP email:", e) flash("Failed to send OTP email.", "warning") return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) # 2) RESET PASSWORD USING OTP elif action == "reset_password": email = request.form.get("email", "").strip().lower() otp = request.form.get("otp", "").strip() new_pass = request.form.get("new_password", "").strip() confirm_pass = request.form.get("confirm_password", "").strip() # basic checks if not (email and otp and new_pass and confirm_pass): flash("Please fill in all fields.", "danger") return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) if new_pass != confirm_pass: flash("New password and confirm password do not match.", "danger") return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) # check OTP from session sess_email = session.get("admin_fp_email") sess_otp = session.get("admin_fp_otp") sess_time = session.get("admin_fp_time") if not (sess_email and sess_otp and sess_time): flash("OTP session expired or not requested. Please request OTP again.", "danger") return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) # email must match the one that requested OTP if email != sess_email: flash("This email does not match the one used to request OTP.", "danger") return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) # check OTP validity (10 minutes) if time.time() - sess_time > 600: # 600 seconds = 10 minutes flash("OTP has expired. Please request a new one.", "danger") # clear OTP session session.pop("admin_fp_email", None) session.pop("admin_fp_otp", None) session.pop("admin_fp_time", None) return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) if otp != sess_otp: flash("Invalid OTP.", "danger") return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) # OTP valid → update password cur.execute(""" SELECT * FROM admins WHERE LOWER(admin_email) = %s """, (email,)) admin = cur.fetchone() if not admin: flash("Admin not found for this email.", "danger") return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email) hashed = generate_password_hash(new_pass) cur.execute(""" UPDATE admins SET admin_password = %s WHERE id = %s """, (hashed, admin["id"])) con.commit() # clear OTP session session.pop("admin_fp_email", None) session.pop("admin_fp_otp", None) session.pop("admin_fp_time", None) flash("Password reset successful. Please log in with your new password.", "success") return redirect(url_for("admin_login")) # GET request return render_template("admin_forgot_password.html", email=email_prefill) # =================================================================== # ADMIN DASHBOARD + ADD STAFF #================================================================ #admin dashboard # ============================ @app.route("/admin-dashboard", methods=["GET", "POST"]) def admin_dashboard(): if "admin_id" not in session: return redirect("/admin-login") con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) admin_id = session["admin_id"] admin_name = session.get("admin_name") # ✅ Get hospital for admin cur.execute(""" SELECT h.hospital_name, h.id AS hospital_id FROM admins a JOIN hospitals h ON h.id = a.hospital_id WHERE a.id = %s """, (admin_id,)) row = cur.fetchone() hospital_name = row["hospital_name"] if row else "Unknown" hospital_id = row["hospital_id"] if row else None # ✅ Get admin department info cur.execute(""" SELECT d.department_name, d.id AS department_id FROM admins a LEFT JOIN departments d ON a.department_id = d.id WHERE a.id = %s """, (admin_id,)) dept_row = cur.fetchone() admin_department = dept_row["department_name"] if dept_row and dept_row["department_name"] else "General" admin_department_id = dept_row["department_id"] if dept_row else None # ✅ Get all departments for dropdown cur.execute(""" SELECT id, department_name FROM departments WHERE hospital_id = %s ORDER BY department_name """, (hospital_id,)) departments = cur.fetchall() # ---------- HANDLE ADD STAFF ---------- if request.method == "POST": role = request.form.get("role") full_name = request.form.get("full_name", "").strip() gender = request.form.get("gender") email = request.form.get("email", "").strip() phone = request.form.get("phone", "").strip() age = request.form.get("age", "").strip() address = request.form.get("address", "").strip() department_id = request.form.get("department_id") or None username = request.form.get("username", "").strip() password_r = request.form.get("password", "").strip() rfid_uid = request.form.get("rfid_uid", "").strip() # ✅ Basic required validation (STOP if failed) # Determine mode is_enterprise = len(departments) > 0 # Required fields for both modes (department excluded) if not (role and full_name and gender and email and phone and age and address and username and password_r and rfid_uid): flash("All fields are required (including RFID scan).", "danger") con.close() return redirect("/admin-dashboard") # Department required ONLY in enterprise mode if is_enterprise and not department_id: flash("Please select a department.", "danger") con.close() return redirect("/admin-dashboard") # ✅ Validate phone if not phone.isdigit() or len(phone) != 10: flash("Phone must be exactly 10 digits.", "danger") con.close() return redirect("/admin-dashboard") # ✅ Validate role if role not in ("Receptionist", "Nurse", "Doctor", "Receptionist + Nurse"): flash("Invalid role selected.", "danger") con.close() return redirect("/admin-dashboard") # ✅ Validate age try: age_int = int(age) except ValueError: flash("Age must be a number.", "danger") con.close() return redirect("/admin-dashboard") password_hash = generate_password_hash(password_r) # ✅ Insert staff try: cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO staff_members (admin_id, department_id, role, full_name, gender, email, phone, age, address, username, rfid_uid, password) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s) """, (admin_id, department_id, role, full_name, gender, email, phone, age_int, address, username, rfid_uid, password_hash)) con.commit() except Exception as e: # ✅ If duplicate email/username/rfid_uid etc. print("DB Insert Error:", e) flash("Duplicate entry detected (Email / Username / RFID already exists).", "danger") con.close() return redirect("/admin-dashboard") # ✅ Send login email try: if role == "Receptionist": login_url = url_for("receptionist_login", _external=True) elif role == "Nurse": login_url = url_for("nurse_login", _external=True) elif role == "Receptionist + Nurse": login_url = url_for("staff.receptionist_nurse_login", _external=True) else: login_url = url_for("doctor_login", _external=True) subject = f"Your {role} Login Credentials" body = f""" Dear {full_name}, You have been added as a {role} at {hospital_name}. Login URL : {login_url} Username : {username} Password : {password_r} Please log in and change your password after first login. Regards, Hospital Management System """ send_email(email, subject, body) flash(f"{role} added and credentials sent via email.", "success") except Exception as e: print("Error sending staff email:", e) flash(f"{role} added, but email sending failed.", "warning") con.close() return redirect("/admin-dashboard") # ---------- SHOW STAFF LIST (GET) ---------- # ✅ IMPORTANT: department_name aliased as department (matches HTML: s.department) cur.execute(""" SELECT sm.id, sm.role, sm.full_name, d.department_name AS department, sm.email, sm.phone, sm.username, sm.is_active, sm.can_login, DATE_FORMAT(sm.created_at, '%d-%m-%Y %H:%i') AS created_on FROM staff_members sm LEFT JOIN departments d ON sm.department_id = d.id WHERE sm.admin_id = %s ORDER BY sm.id DESC """, (admin_id,)) staff_list = cur.fetchall() con.close() context = { "admin_name": admin_name, "hospital_name": hospital_name, "staff_list": staff_list, "admin_department": admin_department, "admin_department_id": admin_department_id, "user_type": "dept_admin" if admin_department_id else "hospital_admin", "departments": departments } return render_template("admin_dashboard.html", **context) # ============================ # ADMIN → EDIT STAFF # ============================ @app.route("/admin-staff/edit/", methods=["GET", "POST"]) def admin_edit_staff(staff_id): if "admin_id" not in session: return redirect("/admin-login") con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) admin_id = session["admin_id"] user_type = session.get("user_type", "admin") # optional # simple validation try: age_int = int(age) except ValueError: flash("Age must be a number.", "danger") return render_template("admin_edit_staff.html", staff=staff) if not phone.isdigit() or len(phone) != 10: flash("Phone must be 10 digits.", "danger") return render_template("admin_edit_staff.html", staff=staff) # Update staff record (no admin_id constraint in WHERE clause) if password: password_hash = generate_password_hash(password) cur.execute(""" UPDATE staff_members SET role=%s, full_name=%s, gender=%s, email=%s, phone=%s, age=%s, address=%s, department=%s, username=%s, password=%s WHERE id=%s """, (role, full_name, gender, email, phone, age_int, address, department, username, password_hash, staff_id)) else: cur.execute(""" UPDATE staff_members SET role=%s, full_name=%s, gender=%s, email=%s, phone=%s, age=%s, address=%s, department=%s, username=%s WHERE id=%s """, (role, full_name, gender, email, phone, age_int, address, department, username, staff_id)) con.commit() flash("Staff updated successfully.", "success") return redirect("/admin-dashboard") return render_template("admin_edit_staff.html", staff=staff) # ============================ # ADMIN → BLOCK / UNBLOCK STAFF # ============================ @app.route("/admin-staff/toggle/") def admin_toggle_staff(staff_id): if "admin_id" not in session: return redirect("/admin-login") con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) admin_id = session["admin_id"] user_type = session.get("user_type", "admin") # Your OTP is: {otp} This OTP is valid for 10 minutes. If you did not request this, ignore this email. Regards, Hospital Management System """ send_email(email, subject, body) flash("OTP has been sent to your email.", "success") except Exception as e: print("Error sending rep OTP email:", e) flash("Failed to send OTP email.", "warning") return render_template("rep_forgot_password.html", email=email) # 2) RESET PASSWORD USING OTP elif action == "reset_password": email = request.form.get("email", "").strip().lower() otp = request.form.get("otp", "").strip() new_pass = request.form.get("new_password", "").strip() confirm_pass = request.form.get("confirm_password", "").strip() if not (email and otp and new_pass and confirm_pass): flash("Please fill in all fields.", "danger") return render_template("rep_forgot_password.html", email=email) if new_pass != confirm_pass: flash("New password and confirm password do not match.", "danger") return render_template("rep_forgot_password.html", email=email) # Session data: sess_email = session.get("rep_fp_email") sess_otp = session.get("rep_fp_otp") sess_time = session.get("rep_fp_time") if not (sess_email and sess_otp and sess_time): flash("OTP session expired or not requested. Please request OTP again.", "danger") return render_template("rep_forgot_password.html", email=email) if email != sess_email: flash("This email does not match the one used to request OTP.", "danger") return render_template("rep_forgot_password.html", email=email) if time.time() - sess_time > 600: flash("OTP has expired. Please request a new one.", "danger") session.pop("rep_fp_email", None) session.pop("rep_fp_otp", None) session.pop("rep_fp_time", None) return render_template("rep_forgot_password.html", email=email) if otp != sess_otp: flash("Invalid OTP.", "danger") return render_template("rep_forgot_password.html", email=email) # All good, update password cur.execute(""" SELECT * FROM hospital_representatives WHERE LOWER(rep_email) = %s """, (email,)) rep = cur.fetchone() if not rep: flash("Representative not found for this email.", "danger") return render_template("rep_forgot_password.html", email=email) hashed = generate_password_hash(new_pass) cur.execute(""" UPDATE hospital_representatives SET rep_password = %s WHERE id = %s """, (hashed, rep['id'])) con.commit() # Clear OTP session session.pop("rep_fp_email", None) session.pop("rep_fp_otp", None) session.pop("rep_fp_time", None) flash("Password reset successful. Please log in with your new password.", "success") return redirect(url_for("rep_login")) return render_template("rep_forgot_password.html", email=email_prefill) #logout @app.route("/rep-logout") def rep_logout(): session.pop("rep_id", None) session.pop("rep_name", None) session.pop("rep_hospital_name", None) return redirect("/rep-login") # Check if user is logged in as admin or staff (nurse) if "admin_id" not in session and "staff_id" not in session: return redirect("/admin-login") con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # Get user details and role admin_id = session.get("admin_id") staff_id = session.get("staff_id") staff_role = session.get("staff_role") # Determine user role user_role = "admin" if admin_id else "nurse" # For staff, only allow nurses to access # For staff (only when NOT admin), only allow nurses if (not admin_id) and staff_id and (staff_role != "Nurse"): flash("Access denied. Only nurses can access bed management.", "danger") return redirect("/staff-dashboard") print(f"User Role: {user_role}, Admin ID: {admin_id}, Staff ID: {staff_id}") # Find hospital for the user hospital_id = None if admin_id: cur.execute("SELECT hospital_id FROM admins WHERE id = %s", (admin_id,)) result = cur.fetchone() hospital_id = result["hospital_id"] if result else None elif staff_id: cur.execute("SELECT sm.hospital_id FROM staff_members sm INNER JOIN admins a ON sm.admin_id = a.id WHERE sm.id = %s", (staff_id,)) result = cur.fetchone() hospital_id = result["hospital_id"] if result else None if not hospital_id: con.close() flash("Hospital not found for your account.", "danger") return redirect("/admin-dashboard" if admin_id else "/nurse-dashboard") # Fetch departments created by HR for this hospital cur.execute(""" SELECT id, department_name, description FROM departments WHERE hospital_id = %s ORDER BY department_name """, (hospital_id,)) departments = cur.fetchall() # Handle POST - Only ADMIN can add beds if request.method == "POST": if user_role != "admin": flash("Only administrators can add beds.", "danger") return redirect(url_for("bed_management")) department_id = request.form.get("department_id") floor_number = request.form.get("floor_number") room_number = request.form.get("room_number") bed_number = request.form.get("bed_number") if not all([department_id, floor_number, room_number, bed_number]): flash("All fields are required.", "danger") else: try: # Check if this bed combination already exists cur.execute(""" SELECT id FROM beds WHERE hospital_id = %s AND department_id = %s AND floor_number = %s AND room_number = %s AND bed_number = %s """, (hospital_id, department_id, floor_number, room_number, bed_number)) existing_bed = cur.fetchone() if existing_bed: flash("A bed with this combination already exists in the selected department.", "warning") else: cur.execute(""" INSERT INTO beds (hospital_id, department_id, floor_number, room_number, bed_number, is_occupied, created_at) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, 0, NOW()) """, (hospital_id, int(department_id), int(floor_number), room_number, bed_number)) con.commit() flash("Bed added successfully!", "success") except Exception as e: con.rollback() flash(f"Error adding bed: {str(e)}", "danger") return redirect(url_for("bed_management")) # Pagination page = int(request.args.get("page", 1)) records_per_page = 10 offset = (page - 1) * records_per_page # Get existing beds with department names (simplified query without nurse tracking for now) cur.execute(""" SELECT b.id, b.bed_number, b.floor_number, b.room_number, b.is_occupied, b.created_at, b.patient_id, d.department_name FROM beds b INNER JOIN departments d ON b.department_id = d.id WHERE b.hospital_id = %s ORDER BY d.department_name, b.floor_number, b.room_number, b.bed_number LIMIT %s OFFSET %s """, (hospital_id, records_per_page, offset)) beds = cur.fetchall() # Get total count for pagination cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as total_beds FROM beds WHERE hospital_id = %s", (hospital_id,)) total_count = cur.fetchone()["total_beds"] total_pages = (total_count + records_per_page - 1) // records_per_page # Get bed statistics cur.execute(""" SELECT COUNT(*) as total_beds, SUM(is_occupied) as occupied_beds, COUNT(*) - SUM(is_occupied) as available_beds FROM beds WHERE hospital_id = %s """, (hospital_id,)) stats = cur.fetchone() or {"total_beds": 0, "occupied_beds": 0, "available_beds": 0} con.close() return render_template( "bed_management.html", departments=departments, beds=beds, page=page, total_pages=total_pages, total_count=total_count, user_role=user_role, stats=stats, records_per_page=records_per_page ) # Route to toggle bed status (simplified for testing) @app.route("/toggle-bed-status/") def toggle_bed_status(bed_id): # Check if user is admin or nurse if "admin_id" not in session and ("staff_id" not in session or session.get("staff_role") != "Nurse"): flash("Access denied.", "danger") return redirect("/admin-login") user_id = session.get("admin_id") or session.get("staff_id") user_type = "admin" if "admin_id" in session else "nurse" con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # Create database connection conn = mysql.connector.connect(**Config.DB_CONFIG) cursor = conn.cursor(dictionary=True) # Query to get all beds with department and patient information query = """ SELECT b.id, b.bed_number, b.floor_number, b.room_number, b.is_occupied, d.department_name, p.full_name as patient_name FROM beds b JOIN departments d ON b.department_id = d.id LEFT JOIN patients p ON b.id = p.assigned_bed_id WHERE b.department_id = %s ORDER BY b.floor_number, b.room_number, b.bed_number """ cursor.execute(query, (department_id,)) beds = cursor.fetchall() print(f"DEBUG: Found {len(beds)} total beds for department {department_id}") beds_data = [] for bed in beds: print(f"Bed: ID={bed['id']}, Floor={bed['floor_number']}, Room={bed['room_number']}, Number={bed['bed_number']}, Occupied={bed['is_occupied']}, Patient={bed['patient_name']}") beds_data.append({ 'id': bed['id'], 'bed_number': bed['bed_number'], 'floor_number': bed['floor_number'], 'room_number': bed['room_number'], 'is_occupied': bool(bed['is_occupied']), 'department_name': bed['department_name'], 'patient_name': bed['patient_name'] if bed['patient_name'] else None }) cursor.close() conn.close() available_count = len([b for b in beds_data if not b['is_occupied']]) print(f"DEBUG: Total beds: {len(beds_data)}, Available: {available_count}") return jsonify({ 'beds': beds_data, 'total_beds': len(beds_data), 'available_beds': available_count }) except Exception as e: print(f"Error in get_bed_list: {str(e)}") import traceback traceback.print_exc() return jsonify({'error': f'Failed to load bed list: {str(e)}'}), 500 # ============================ # RECEPTIONIST FORGOT PASSWORD (EMAIL + OTP + NEW PASSWORD) # ============================ @app.route("/receptionist-forgot-password", methods=["GET", "POST"]) def receptionist_forgot_password(): con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # prefill email if stored in session email_prefill = session.get("receptionist_fp_email", "") if request.method == "POST": action = request.form.get("action") # 1) SEND OTP if action == "send_otp": email = request.form.get("email", "").strip().lower() if not email: flash("Please enter your email.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) # find receptionist by email cur.execute(""" SELECT * FROM staff_members WHERE LOWER(email) = %s AND role = %s """, (email, "Receptionist")) staff = cur.fetchone() if not staff: flash("No receptionist account found with that email.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) # generate 6-digit OTP otp = str(random.randint(100000, 999999)) # store otp + email + timestamp in session (10 minutes) session["receptionist_fp_email"] = email session["receptionist_fp_otp"] = otp session["receptionist_fp_time"] = time.time() # send OTP email try: subject = "Receptionist Password Reset OTP" body = f""" Dear {staff['full_name']}, You requested to reset your receptionist account password. Your OTP is: {otp} This OTP is valid for 10 minutes. If you did not request this, please ignore this email. Regards, Hospital Management System """ send_email(email, subject, body) flash("OTP has been sent to your email.", "success") except Exception as e: print("Error sending receptionist OTP email:", e) flash("Failed to send OTP email.", "warning") return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) # 2) RESET PASSWORD USING OTP elif action == "reset_password": email = request.form.get("email", "").strip().lower() otp = request.form.get("otp", "").strip() new_pass = request.form.get("new_password", "").strip() confirm_pass = request.form.get("confirm_password", "").strip() if not (email and otp and new_pass and confirm_pass): flash("Please fill in all fields.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) if new_pass != confirm_pass: flash("New password and confirm password do not match.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) # check data saved in session sess_email = session.get("receptionist_fp_email") sess_otp = session.get("receptionist_fp_otp") sess_time = session.get("receptionist_fp_time") if not (sess_email and sess_otp and sess_time): flash("OTP session expired or not requested. Please request OTP again.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) if email != sess_email: flash("This email does not match the one used to request OTP.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) if time.time() - sess_time > 600: flash("OTP has expired. Please request a new one.", "danger") session.pop("receptionist_fp_email", None) session.pop("receptionist_fp_otp", None) session.pop("receptionist_fp_time", None) return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) if otp != sess_otp: flash("Invalid OTP.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) # OTP valid -> update password cur.execute(""" SELECT * FROM staff_members WHERE LOWER(email) = %s AND role = %s """, (email, "Receptionist")) staff = cur.fetchone() if not staff: flash("Receptionist not found for this email.", "danger") return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email) hashed = generate_password_hash(new_pass) cur.execute(""" UPDATE staff_members SET password = %s WHERE id = %s """, (hashed, staff["id"])) con.commit() # clear session session.pop("receptionist_fp_email", None) session.pop("receptionist_fp_otp", None) session.pop("receptionist_fp_time", None) flash("Password reset successful. Please log in with your new password.", "success") return redirect(url_for("receptionist_login")) # GET request return render_template("receptionist_forgot_password.html", email=email_prefill) # === CONSULTANCY RECORDS PAGE - ONLY DOCTOR'S OWN RECORDS === @app.route('/consultancy_records') def consultancy_records(): if 'staff_id' not in session: return redirect(url_for('doctor_login')) # === EDIT CONSULTANCY - SECURITY CHECK === @app.route('/edit-consultancy/', methods=['GET', 'POST']) # Changed underscore to hyphen def edit_consultancy(id): if 'staff_id' not in session: return redirect(url_for('doctor_login')) con = get_db() cursor = con.cursor(dictionary=True) doctor_id = session['staff_id'] if request.method == 'GET': # Security: Double-check doctor ownership before updating cursor.execute("SELECT doctor_id FROM consultancy_records WHERE id = %s", (id,)) result = cursor.fetchone() if not result or result['doctor_id'] != doctor_id: cursor.close() con.close() return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Access denied'}), 403 cursor.execute(""" UPDATE consultancy_records SET chief_complaint = %s, diagnosis = %s, prescription = %s, additional_notes = %s, appointment_datetime = %s WHERE id = %s AND doctor_id = %s """, ( data['chief_complaint'], data['diagnosis'], data['prescription'], data['additional_notes'], data['appointment_datetime'], id, doctor_id )) con.commit() cursor.close() con.close() return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Record updated successfully'}) except Exception as e: print(f"Error updating consultancy: {e}") return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 500 # ds.doctor_id AND ds.schedule_date >= CURDATE() AND ds.schedule_date <= DATE_ADD(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 7 DAY) WHERE s.role = 'Doctor' AND s.is_active = 1 AND EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM admins a WHERE except Exception as e: @app.route("/api/doctor-status") def api_doctor_status(): if "staff_id" not in session: return jsonify({"error": "Unauthorized"}), 401 admin_id = session.get("admin_id") if not admin_id: return jsonify({"error": "Missing admin_id in session"}), 400 con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) cur.execute(""" SELECT id, full_name, phone, department_id, is_available, last_status_update FROM staff_members WHERE admin_id = %s AND role = 'Doctor' AND is_active = 1 ORDER BY is_available DESC, full_name ASC """, (admin_id,)) doctors = cur.fetchall() con.close() return jsonify({"doctors": doctors}) @app.route("/doctor-status", methods=["GET"]) def doctor_status_api(): if "admin_id" not in session: return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}), 401 hospital_id = session.get("hospital_id") if not hospital_id: return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "Hospital ID missing in session"}), 401 now = datetime.now() today = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") now_time = now.strftime("%H:%M:%S") conn = get_db_connection() cur = conn.cursor(dictionary=True) # All doctors for this admin's hospital cur.execute(""" SELECT sm.id, sm.full_name, sm.email, sm.phone, sm.username, sm.is_active, sm.can_login, d.department_name FROM staff_members sm JOIN admins a ON a.id = sm.admin_id LEFT JOIN departments d ON d.id = sm.department_id WHERE a.hospital_id = %s AND sm.role = 'Doctor' ORDER BY sm.full_name """, (hospital_id,)) doctors = cur.fetchall() # Busy doctors RIGHT NOW (in an active available schedule slot) cur.execute(""" SELECT DISTINCT doctor_id FROM doctor_schedules WHERE hospital_id = %s AND schedule_date = %s AND is_active = 1 AND is_available = 1 AND start_time <= %s AND end_time > %s """, (hospital_id, today, now_time, now_time)) busy_ids = {r["doctor_id"] for r in cur.fetchall()} # Optional: get today's shifts for each doctor (small summary) cur.execute(""" SELECT doctor_id, start_time, end_time, shift_type FROM doctor_schedules WHERE hospital_id = %s AND schedule_date = %s AND is_active = 1 ORDER BY start_time """, (hospital_id, today)) shifts = cur.fetchall() shift_map = {} for s in shifts: shift_map.setdefault(s["doctor_id"], []).append({ "start_time": str(s["start_time"]), "end_time": str(s["end_time"]), "shift_type": s["shift_type"] }) for doc in doctors: if not doc["is_active"] or not doc["can_login"]: doc["status"] = "INACTIVE" else: doc["status"] = "BUSY" if doc["id"] in busy_ids else "FREE" doc["today_shifts"] = shift_map.get(doc["id"], []) cur.close() conn.close() return jsonify({ "success": True, "date": today, "time": now_time, "doctors": doctors }) # --------------------------------doctor shedule # ============================ # RECEPTIONIST → TOGGLE PATIENT STATUS (BLOCK / UNBLOCK) # ============================ @app.route("/receptionist-patient/toggle/", methods=["POST"]) def receptionist_toggle_patient(pid): if session.get("staff_role") != "Receptionist": return redirect("/receptionist-login") staff_id = session.get("staff_id") con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # make sure this patient belongs to this receptionist cur.execute(""" SELECT id, is_active FROM patients WHERE id = %s AND created_by_staff_id = %s """, (pid, staff_id)) patient = cur.fetchone() if not patient: flash("Patient not found.", "danger") return redirect(url_for("receptionist_dashboard")) new_status = 0 if patient["is_active"] == 1 else 1 cur = con.cursor() cur.execute(""" UPDATE patients SET is_active = %s WHERE id = %s AND created_by_staff_id = %s """, (new_status, pid, staff_id)) con.commit() flash("Patient status updated.", "success") return redirect(url_for("receptionist_dashboard")) @app.route("/rep-staff") def rep_staff(): if "rep_id" not in session: return redirect("/rep-login") con = get_db() cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # Step 1. Get the current rep's hospital name (use hospital_representatives) cur.execute(""" SELECT h.id AS hospital_id, h.hospital_name FROM hospital_representatives hr JOIN hospitals h ON hr.hospital_id = h.id WHERE hr.id = %s """, (session["rep_id"],)) result = cur.fetchone() if not result: flash("Invalid session or hospital.") return redirect("/rep-login") hospital_id = result["hospital_id"] hospital_name = result["hospital_name"] # Step 2. Get all staff (grouping by admin) in this hospital, with stats cur.execute(""" SELECT s.id AS staff_id, s.role, s.full_name, s.email, s.phone, s.username, s.department_id, s.is_active AS staff_active, DATE_FORMAT(s.created_at, '%d-%m-%Y %H:%i') AS staff_created_on, a.id AS admin_id, a.admin_name, a.admin_username, a.admin_email, a.admin_phone, a.department_id AS admin_department, a.is_active AS admin_active, DATE_FORMAT(a.created_at, '%d-%m-%Y %H:%i') AS admin_created_on, COALESCE(pc.patient_count, 0) AS patient_count, COALESCE(vc.vitals_count, 0) AS vitals_count, COALESCE(cc.consultation_count, 0) AS consultation_count FROM staff_members s JOIN admins a ON s.admin_id = a.id LEFT JOIN ( SELECT created_by_staff_id, COUNT(*) AS patient_count FROM patients GROUP BY created_by_staff_id ) pc ON s.id = pc.created_by_staff_id AND s.role = 'Receptionist' LEFT JOIN ( SELECT recorded_by_staff_id, COUNT(*) AS vitals_count FROM patient_vitals GROUP BY recorded_by_staff_id ) vc ON s.id = vc.recorded_by_staff_id AND s.role = 'Nurse' LEFT JOIN ( SELECT doctor_id, COUNT(*) AS consultation_count FROM consultancy_records GROUP BY doctor_id ) cc ON s.id = cc.doctor_id AND s.role = 'Doctor' WHERE a.rep_id = %s ORDER BY a.admin_name, s.role, s.full_name """, (hospital_id,)) staff_rows = cur.fetchall() # Step 3. Calculate admin statistics (optional) admin_stats = {} for row in staff_rows: admin_name = row['admin_name'] if admin_name not in admin_stats: admin_stats[admin_name] = { 'total_patients': 0, 'total_vitals': 0, 'total_consultations': 0, 'receptionist_count': 0, 'nurse_count': 0, 'doctor_count': 0 } # Add counts based on role if row['role'] == 'Receptionist': admin_stats[admin_name]['total_patients'] += row['patient_count'] admin_stats[admin_name]['receptionist_count'] += 1 elif row['role'] == 'Nurse': admin_stats[admin_name]['total_vitals'] += row['vitals_count'] admin_stats[admin_name]['nurse_count'] += 1 elif row['role'] == 'Doctor': admin_stats[admin_name]['total_consultations'] += row['consultation_count'] admin_stats[admin_name]['doctor_count'] += 1 # Step 4. Get detailed patient info for this hospital only cur.execute(""" SELECT p.id, p.full_name, p.age, p.gender, p.rfid_uid, p.is_active, p.created_by_staff_id, DATE_FORMAT(p.created_at, '%d-%m-%Y %H:%i') AS created_at, s.full_name AS added_by_staff, a.admin_name FROM patients p JOIN staff_members s ON p.created_by_staff_id = s.id JOIN admins a ON s.admin_id = a.id WHERE p.hospital_id = %s ORDER BY p.created_at DESC """, (hospital_id,)) patients = cur.fetchall() receptionist_patients = {} for pat in patients: rid = pat['created_by_staff_id'] receptionist_patients.setdefault(rid, []).append(pat) # Step 5. Get detailed vitals info for nurses in this hospital cur.execute(""" SELECT pv.recorded_by_staff_id as nurse_id, pv.patient_id, p.full_name as patient_name, p.rfid_uid, DATE_FORMAT(pv.created_at, '%d-%m-%Y %H:%i') as vitals_time, pv.temperature, pv.bp_systolic, pv.bp_diastolic, pv.pulse, pv.sp_o2, s.full_name AS nurse_name, a.admin_name FROM patient_vitals pv JOIN patients p ON pv.patient_id = p.id JOIN staff_members s ON pv.recorded_by_staff_id = s.id JOIN admins a ON s.admin_id = a.id WHERE a.rep_id = %s ORDER BY pv.created_at DESC """, (hospital_id,)) vitals = cur.fetchall() nurse_vitals = {} for vt in vitals: nid = vt['nurse_id'] nurse_vitals.setdefault(nid, []).append(vt) # Step 6. Get detailed consultations for doctors in this hospital cur.execute(""" SELECT c.doctor_id, c.patient_id, p.full_name as patient_name, p.rfid_uid, c.chief_complaint, c.diagnosis, c.prescription, DATE_FORMAT(c.created_at, '%d-%m-%Y %H:%i') as consultation_time, s.full_name AS doctor_name, s.department_id AS doctor_department, a.admin_name FROM consultancy_records c JOIN patients p ON c.patient_id = p.id JOIN staff_members s ON c.doctor_id = s.id JOIN admins a ON s.admin_id = a.id WHERE a.rep_id = %s ORDER BY c.created_at DESC """, (hospital_id,)) consults = cur.fetchall() doctor_consultancy = {} for cc in consults: did = cc['doctor_id'] doctor_consultancy.setdefault(did, []).append(cc) con.close() return render_template( "rep_staff_comprehensive.html", staff_rows=staff_rows, admin_stats=admin_stats, receptionist_patients=receptionist_patients, nurse_vitals=nurse_vitals, doctor_consultancy=doctor_consultancy ) # Please request a new one.", "danger") # clear OTP session session.pop("nurse_fp_email", None) session.pop("nurse_fp_otp", None) session.pop("nurse_fp_time", None) return render_template("nurse_forgot_password.html", email=email) if otp != sess_otp: flash("Invalid OTP.", "danger") return render_template("nurse_forgot_password.html", email=email) # OTP valid → update password cur.execute(""" SELECT * FROM nurses WHERE LOWER(nurse_email) = %s """, (email,)) nurse = cur.fetchone() if not nurse: flash("Nurse not found for this email.", "danger") return render_template("nurse_forgot_password.html", email=email) hashed = generate_password_hash(new_pass) cur.execute(""" UPDATE nurses SET nurse_password = %s WHERE id = %s """, (hashed, nurse["id"])) con.commit() # clear OTP session session.pop("nurse_fp_email", None) session.pop("nurse_fp_otp", None) session.pop("nurse_fp_time", None) flash("Password reset successful. cur.fetchall() # Handle department filter department_id = request.args.get('department_id') # Pagination page = int(request.args.get("page", 1)) records_per_page = 15 offset = (page - 1) * records_per_page # Build query with optional department filter if department_id: bed_query = """ SELECT b.id, b.bed_number, b.floor_number, b.room_number, b.is_occupied, b.created_at, b.patient_id, d.department_name FROM beds b INNER JOIN departments d ON b.department_id = d.id WHERE b.hospital_id = %s AND b.department_id = %s ORDER BY d.department_name, b.floor_number, b.room_number, b.bed_number LIMIT %s OFFSET %s """ count_query = "SELECT COUNT(*) as total_beds FROM beds WHERE hospital_id = %s AND department_id = %s" cur.execute(bed_query, (hospital_id, department_id, records_per_page, offset)) beds = cur.fetchall() cur.execute(count_query, (hospital_id, department_id)) else: bed_query = """ SELECT b.id, b.bed_number, b.floor_number, b.room_number, b.is_occupied, b.created_at, b.patient_id, d.department_name FROM beds b INNER JOIN departments d ON b.department_id = d.id WHERE b.hospital_id = %s ORDER BY d.department_name, b.floor_number, b.room_number, b.bed_number LIMIT %s OFFSET %s """ count_query = "SELECT COUNT(*) as total_beds FROM beds WHERE hospital_id = %s" cur.execute(bed_query, (hospital_id, records_per_page, offset)) beds = cur.fetchall() cur.execute(count_query, (hospital_id,)) total_count = cur.fetchone()["total_beds"] total_pages = (total_count + records_per_page - 1) // records_per_page con.close() return render_template( mysql.connector.connect(**Config.DB_CONFIG) cur = con.cursor(dictionary=True) # Get current staff's hospital patients with their latest vitals cur.execute(""" SELECT p.id AS patient_id, p.full_name AS patient_name, p.age, p.gender, p.phone, p.email, p.address, pv.temperature, pv.bp_systolic, pv.bp_diastolic, pv.pulse, pv.respiratory_rate, pv.sp_o2, pv.weight, pv.height, pv.blood_sugar, pv.pain_score, pv.additional_notes, DATE_FORMAT(pv.created_at, '%d-%m-%Y %H:%i') AS created_at, s.full_name as recorded_by_staff, s.role as staff_role FROM patients p LEFT JOIN ( SELECT pv1.* FROM ON p.id = pv.patient_id LEFT JOIN staff_members s ON pv.recorded_by_staff_id = s.id JOIN admins a ON a.id = s.admin_id JOIN hospital_representatives hr ON hr.id = a.rep_id JOIN hospitals h ON h.id = hr.hospital_id WHERE p.hospital_id = h.id AND s.id = %s ORDER BY p.created_at DESC """, (session["staff_id"],)) patients = cur.fetchall() cur.close() con.close() return render_template("view_patients.html", patients=patients) @app.route('/admin/patient-tracking') def admin_patient_tracking(): if 'admin_id' not in session: return redirect(url_for('admin_login')) admin_hospital = session['admin_hospital_name'] try: connection = mysql.connector.connect(**Config.DB_CONFIG) cursor = connection.cursor(dictionary=True) query = """ SELECT p.id as patient_id, p.full_name, p.age, p.gender, p.phone, p.email, p.rfid_uid, p.created_at, s.full_name as added_by_staff, s.role as added_by_role, d.department_name as staff_department, s.is_active as staff_is_active, a.admin_name as admin_name, -- Count vitals (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM patient_vitals pv WHERE pv.patient_id = p.id) as vitals_count, -- Count consultations (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM consultancy_records cr WHERE cr.patient_id = p.id) as consultation_count, -- Latest vitals info (SELECT s2.full_name FROM patient_vitals pv2 LEFT JOIN staff_members s2 ON pv2.recorded_by_staff_id = s2.id WHERE pv2.patient_id = p.id ORDER BY pv2.created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_vitals_by, (SELECT s2.role FROM patient_vitals pv2 LEFT JOIN staff_members s2 ON pv2.recorded_by_staff_id = s2.id WHERE pv2.patient_id = p.id ORDER BY pv2.created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_vitals_by_role, (SELECT pv2.created_at FROM patient_vitals pv2 WHERE pv2.patient_id = p.id ORDER BY pv2.created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_vitals_date, -- Latest nurse Online/Offline status for vitals (SELECT s2.is_active FROM patient_vitals pv2 LEFT JOIN staff_members s2 ON pv2.recorded_by_staff_id = s2.id WHERE pv2.patient_id = p.id ORDER BY pv2.created_at DESC LIMIT 1) AS last_vitals_staff_is_active, -- Latest consultation info (SELECT s3.full_name FROM consultancy_records cr2 LEFT JOIN staff_members s3 ON cr2.doctor_id = s3.id WHERE cr2.patient_id = p.id ORDER BY cr2.created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_consultation_by, (SELECT d3.department_name FROM consultancy_records cr2 LEFT JOIN staff_members s3 ON cr2.doctor_id = s3.id LEFT JOIN departments d3 ON s3.department_id = d3.id WHERE cr2.patient_id = p.id ORDER BY cr2.created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_consultation_dept, (SELECT cr2.created_at FROM consultancy_records cr2 WHERE cr2.patient_id = p.id ORDER BY cr2.created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_consultation_date, -- Latest doctor Online/Offline status for consult (SELECT s3.is_active FROM consultancy_records cr2 LEFT JOIN staff_members s3 ON cr2.doctor_id = s3.id WHERE cr2.patient_id = p.id ORDER BY cr2.created_at DESC LIMIT 1) AS last_consultation_doctor_is_active FROM patients p LEFT JOIN staff_members s ON p.created_by_staff_id = s.id LEFT JOIN departments d ON s.department_id = d.id LEFT JOIN admins a ON s.admin_id = a.id LEFT JOIN hospitals h ON p.hospital_id = h.id WHERE h.hospital_name = %s ORDER BY p.created_at DESC """ cursor.execute(query, (admin_hospital,)) patients = cursor.fetchall() for patient in patients: patient['vitals_count'] = int(patient.get('vitals_count', 0) or 0) patient['consultation_count'] = int(patient.get('consultation_count', 0) or 0) patient['last_vitals_staff_is_active'] = int(patient.get('last_vitals_staff_is_active', 0) or 0) patient['last_consultation_doctor_is_active'] = int(patient.get('last_consultation_doctor_is_active', 0) or 0) cursor.close() connection.close() return render_template('admin_patient_tracking.html', patients=patients) except Exception as e: print(f"Error in admin_patient_tracking: {e}") return f"Database error: {e}", 500 # @app.route('/admin/patient-tracking') # def admin_patient_tracking(): # if
